# OBRÁZKY — jedno schéma ke každé otázce

**Ke každé ze 136 zkouškových otázek jeden obrázek, který nese její hlavní myšlenku.**
Není to náhrada textu — je to to, co si máš vybavit, když se u zkoušky zasekneš.
Text ke stejným otázkám je ve `VYCUC-FINAL`.

> **Jak to číst:** ⚠️ **červený rámeček = past nebo nežádoucí účinek** ·
> **zelený rámeček = jádro věci** · **červená věta pod obrázkem = co si z něj odnést.**

---

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 224" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Registruje SÚKL / EMA</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Smí tvrdit, že léčí nemoc</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Hlásí se nežádoucí účinky</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">DOPLNĚK STRAVY</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Účinnost prokazovat NEMUSÍ</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Jen ohlášení na SZPI — potravina</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Nesmí tvrdit, že léčí</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Pacient v tom rozdíl nevidí</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">Zdravotnický prostředek působí FYZIKÁLNĚ (výplň, implantát, obvaz), lék FARMAKOLOGICKY.</text></svg>

## O4 · Preklinické a klinické hodnocení léčiv

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 477" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="477" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Proč se vzácný nežádoucí účinek najde až po uvedení na trh</text><rect x="96" y="56" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="80" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#556964" text-anchor="start">PREKLINIKA</text><text x="110" y="101" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">zkumavka a zvíře — toxicita, kinetika, mechanismus</text><rect x="96" y="134" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="158" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">FÁZE I · desítky ZDRAVÝCH dobrovolníků</text><text x="110" y="179" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">ptá se: JE TO BEZPEČNÉ a jak se to chová?</text><rect x="96" y="212" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="236" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">FÁZE II · stovky NEMOCNÝCH</text><text x="110" y="257" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">ptá se: ZABÍRÁ TO a v jaké dávce?</text><rect x="96" y="290" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="314" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="start">FÁZE III · tisíce nemocných, srovnání</text><text x="110" y="335" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">ptá se: JE TO LEPŠÍ než dosavadní léčba?</text><rect x="96" y="368" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="392" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">FÁZE IV · po registraci, celá populace</text><text x="110" y="413" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">⚠️ tady se najdou VZÁCNÉ nežádoucí účinky</text><line x1="58" y1="62" x2="58" y2="430" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="30" y="245" font-size="12" fill="#556964" transform="rotate(-90 30 245)" text-anchor="middle">čas a počet lidí</text><text x="450" y="456" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">Fáze III má tisíce pacientů — účinek, který se stane jednomu z deseti tisíc, v ní nemá šanci vyjít najevo.</text></svg>

## O5 · Způsoby aplikace léčiv, výhody a nevýhody

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 224" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="224" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Hlavní rozdíl není rychlost, ale jestli lék projde játry</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">PERORÁLNĚ (ústy)</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Pohodlné, levné, bezpečné</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Prochází JÁTRY = first-pass</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Pomalejší a méně předvídatelné</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Nejde použít u zvracení a bezvědomí</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">PARENTERÁLNĚ (i.v., i.m., s.c.)</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ i.v. = 100% dostupnost, okamžitě</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Obchází játra i žaludek</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Nutná sterilita a personál</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Podané se nedá vzít zpět</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">Sublingválně, rektálně a transdermálně first-pass také obcházejí — proto nitroglycerin pod jazyk a ne polknout.</text></svg>

## O6 · Lékové formy — perorální a orální

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 224" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="224" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Dvě slova, která znějí stejně a znamenají opak</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">PERORÁLNÍ = k POLKNUTÍ</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Tablety, tobolky, sirupy</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Účinek až po vstřebání ve střevě</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Enterosolventní obal chrání před žaludkem</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Retardované uvolňují postupně</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">ORÁLNÍ = ÚČINEK V ÚSTECH</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Pastilky, ústní vody, gely</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Sublingválně se vstřebá rovnou do krve</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Obchází játra (nitroglycerin)</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Zubařsky nejbližší skupina</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Retardovanou ani enterosolventní tabletu nikdy nedrtit — zničí se mechanismus a uvolní se celá dávka najednou.</text></svg>

## O7 · Lékové formy — parenterální a dermatologika

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 399" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="399" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Rychlost účinku sleduje prokrvení místa vpichu</text><rect x="96" y="56" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="80" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="start">i.v. — nitrožilně</text><text x="110" y="101" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">účinek OKAMŽITĚ, 100 % dávky v krvi</text><rect x="96" y="134" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="158" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">i.m. — do svalu</text><text x="110" y="179" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">rychle, dobře prokrvený sval</text><rect x="96" y="212" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="236" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">s.c. — pod kůži</text><text x="110" y="257" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">pomaleji, hodí se pro inzulin a hepariny</text><rect x="96" y="290" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="314" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#556964" text-anchor="start">depotní / transdermální náplast</text><text x="110" y="335" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">⚠️ nejpomalejší, ale drží dny</text><line x1="58" y1="62" x2="58" y2="352" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="30" y="206" font-size="12" fill="#556964" transform="rotate(-90 30 206)" text-anchor="middle">čím níž, tím pomaleji</text><text x="450" y="378" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">Masti (tučné, na suchou kůži) · krémy (voda i tuk) · gely (vodné, chladí) — čím tučnější základ, tím hlubší průnik.</text></svg>

## O8 · Lékové formy — oční, ušní, nosní, rektalia, vaginalia, inhalanda

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 313" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="313" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Každá „místní" forma se dokáže vstřebat do celého těla</text><rect x="328" y="101" width="244" height="124" rx="14" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2"/><text x="450" y="150" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">MÍSTNÍ PODÁNÍ ⚠️ „místní"</text><text x="450" y="167" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">neznamená „bez celkového</text><text x="450" y="184" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">účinku"</text><rect x="26" y="56" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="83" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">OČNÍ kapky — timolol z kapek</text><text x="139" y="99" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">sráží tep a dusí astmatika</text><line x1="258" y1="87" x2="322" y2="136" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="26" y="132" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="159" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">NOSNÍ kapky — ⚠️ max 7 dní,</text><text x="139" y="175" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">jinak rhinitis medicamentosa</text><line x1="258" y1="163" x2="322" y2="163" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="26" y="208" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="235" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">INHALANDA — kortikoid → ⚠️</text><text x="139" y="251" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">kandidóza v ústech</text><line x1="258" y1="239" x2="322" y2="190" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="56" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="74" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">REKTÁLNĚ — částečně obchází</text><text x="761" y="91" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">játra, funguje i při</text><text x="761" y="108" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">zvracení</text><line x1="642" y1="87" x2="578" y2="136" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="132" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="159" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">VAGINÁLNĚ — místní léčba</text><text x="761" y="175" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">infekcí</text><line x1="642" y1="163" x2="578" y2="163" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="208" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="235" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">UŠNÍ — ⚠️ ne při perforaci</text><text x="761" y="251" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">bubínku</text><line x1="642" y1="239" x2="578" y2="190" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="450" y="292" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">Po nakapání do oka stiskni vnitřní koutek — sníží to vstřebání přes slzný kanálek do nosu.</text></svg>

## O9 · Komunikace, adherence, compliance, placebo a nocebo

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 224" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="224" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Tvoje slova jsou účinná látka s dávkou</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">PLACEBO</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Očekávání ZLEPŠENÍ → stav se zlepší</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Měřitelný, ne „vymyšlený" efekt</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Zesiluje účinek každého skutečného léku</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Vzhled a jistota lékaře na tom mají podíl</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">NOCEBO</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Očekávání ŠKODY → potíže se objeví</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Vzniká i z příbalového letáku</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Časté vysazení statinů a antidepresiv</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Vytvoří ho i špatně řečená věta</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">Adherence = jestli pacient léčbu dodržuje. ⚠️ Nejčastější příčina „selhání léčby" není špatný lék, ale nebrané tablety.</text></svg>

## O10 · Přechod látek biologickými membránami

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 202" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="202" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Iontová past — nejvýnosnější obrázek celé obecné farmakologie</text><rect x="30" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="107" y="88" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">NENABITÁ forma</text><text x="107" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">tuková, projde</text><text x="107" y="122" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">membránou</text><line x1="192" y1="101" x2="250" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="222" y="92" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">prostá difuze</text><rect x="258" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="336" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">PROJDE do buňky</text><line x1="421" y1="101" x2="479" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="487" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="564" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">uvnitř jiné pH →</text><text x="564" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">NABIJE SE</text><line x1="650" y1="101" x2="708" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="678" y="92" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">IONTOVÁ PAST</text><rect x="716" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="793" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ nabité neprojde</text><text x="793" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">zpět → HROMADÍ SE</text><text x="450" y="166" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">Vysvětlí pětkrát: anestezie nezabírá v zaníceném zubu · alkalizace moči u otravy aspirinem · laktulóza u encefalopatie · léky v</text><text x="450" y="181" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">mléce a u plodu · nikotin z kouře.</text></svg>

## O11 · Základní farmakokinetické parametry a procesy

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 202" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="202" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">ADME — co tělo dělá s lékem</text><rect x="30" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="107" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">A — ABSORPCE</text><text x="107" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">vstřebání</text><line x1="192" y1="101" x2="250" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="258" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="336" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">D — DISTRIBUCE</text><text x="336" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">rozvod po těle</text><line x1="421" y1="101" x2="479" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="487" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="564" y="88" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">M — METABOLISMUS</text><text x="564" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">přeměna, hlavně v</text><text x="564" y="122" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">játrech</text><line x1="650" y1="101" x2="708" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="716" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="793" y="88" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">E — EXKRECE</text><text x="793" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">vyloučení, hlavně</text><text x="793" y="122" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">ledvinami</text><text x="450" y="166" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Farmakokinetika = co tělo dělá s lékem. Farmakodynamika = co lék dělá s tělem. Nepleť si to, ptají se na to jako na první</text><text x="450" y="181" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">otázku.</text></svg>

## O12 · Procesy nultého a prvního řádu, saturační kinetika

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 224" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="224" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Proč u fenytoinu stačí přidat kousek a pacient je otrávený</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">PRVNÍ ŘÁD (většina léků)</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Odbourá se stále stejný PODÍL</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Např. polovina za hodinu</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Enzym má rezervu, stíhá</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Má poločas — dá se počítat</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">NULTÝ ŘÁD (etanol, fenytoin, aspirin ve vysoké</text><text x="666" y="99" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">dávce)</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Odbourá se stále stejné MNOŽSTVÍ</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Enzym je NASYCENÝ, víc nestíhá</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Malé zvýšení dávky → skok hladiny</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Poločas nemá smysl</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">Saturační kinetika = přechod mezi nimi. Dokud je enzym volný, jede první řád; jakmile se zahltí, přepne na nultý.</text></svg>

---

## O13 · Absorpce, Batemanova funkce, biologická dostupnost, AUC

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 224" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="224" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Batemanova křivka — co znamená její vrchol</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">VRCHOL KŘIVKY (Cmax, tmax)</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ NENÍ konec vstřebávání</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Je to REMÍZA: kolik se vstřebá,</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• tolik se právě odbourává</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Potom už převáží eliminace</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">AUC — plocha pod křivkou</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Kolik léku se CELKEM dostalo do krve</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Základ biologické dostupnosti (F)</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ i.v. = F 100 %, p.o. je vždy méně</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Podle AUC se posuzují generika</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">Biologická dostupnost je podíl dávky, který se dostal do krve nezměněný. First-pass ji sráží nejvíc.</text></svg>

---

## O14 · Distribuce, distribuční objem, vazba na bílkoviny, bariéry

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 239" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="239" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Proč pacient s nízkým albuminem dostane při normální dávce toxický účinek</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#F4F7F5" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#C6D5D0" text-anchor="middle">VÁZANÁ frakce</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Sedí na albuminu (kyselé léky)</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• nebo na orosomukoidu (zásadité)</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ NEÚČINNÁ — je to sklad</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Nefiltruje se ani nepůsobí</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">VOLNÁ frakce</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ JEN TA PŮSOBÍ</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Jen ta se metabolizuje a vylučuje</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Málo albuminu (senior, cirhóza, nefrotický sy)</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• → volné frakce přibude → předávkování</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">Distribuční objem není skutečný objem — je to poměr dávky a koncentrace v krvi. Velký Vd = lék je schovaný ve tkáních, dialýza</text><text x="450" y="218" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">ho nedostane ven.</text></svg>

---

## O15 · Eliminace, poločas, fáze α a β, clearance

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 224" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="224" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Dvě fáze poklesu — a jen ta druhá znamená, že lék mizí z těla</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">FÁZE α — DISTRIBUČNÍ</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Rychlý pokles hladiny</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ lék se jen PŘESTĚHOVAL do tkání</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Nic se ještě neodbouralo</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Tím končí účinek thiopentalu</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">FÁZE β — ELIMINAČNÍ</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Pomalý pokles</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Teď se lék skutečně odbourává a vylučuje</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Z ní se počítá biologický poločas</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Po 4–5 poločasech je lék pryč</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">Clearance = objem krve očištěný za jednotku času. Poločas závisí na clearance A na distribučním objemu zároveň.</text></svg>

---

## O16 · Dávkovací režim, kumulace, kumulační index

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 202" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="202" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Nasycovací × udržovací dávka na jednom obrázku</text><rect x="30" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#F4F7F5" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="107" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#556964" text-anchor="middle">VANA = tělo</text><line x1="192" y1="101" x2="250" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="222" y="92" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">napouštím</text><rect x="258" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="336" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">KOHOUTEK =</text><text x="336" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">dávkování</text><line x1="421" y1="101" x2="479" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="450" y="92" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">vypouštím</text><rect x="487" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="564" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">ODTOK = eliminace</text><line x1="650" y1="101" x2="708" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="678" y="92" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">za 4–5 poločasů</text><rect x="716" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="793" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">USTÁLENÝ STAV:</text><text x="793" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">přitéká = odtéká</text><text x="450" y="166" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">NASYCOVACÍ dávka naplní vanu naráz (řídí ji distribuční objem). UDRŽOVACÍ jen dorovnává, co odteče (řídí ji clearance). ⚠️</text><text x="450" y="181" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">Ustálený stav přijde vždy až za 4–5 poločasů.</text></svg>

## O17 · Biotransformace léčiv, fáze, příklady

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 368" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="368" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Fáze I × fáze II na jednom léku — a celá otrava paracetamolem</text><rect x="30" y="137" width="250" height="92" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="155" y="179" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">PARACETAMOL v terapeutické</text><text x="155" y="195" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">dávce</text><line x1="286" y1="183" x2="462" y2="93" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="129" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">90 % — fáze II, konjugace</text><rect x="470" y="56" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">neškodné metabolity → moč</text><line x1="286" y1="183" x2="462" y2="183" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="174" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">10 % — fáze I, CYP2E1</text><rect x="470" y="146" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="187" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ NAPQI — jedovatý</text><line x1="286" y1="183" x2="462" y2="273" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="219" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">GLUTATHION ho zneškodní</text><rect x="470" y="236" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="277" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ dokud zásoba vydrží</text><text x="450" y="332" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">Předávkování, alkohol nebo hladovění → hlavní cesta se nasytí, NAPQI přibývá, glutathion dojde → nekróza jater. Antidotum</text><text x="450" y="347" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">N-ACETYLCYSTEIN dodá stavební kámen glutathionu.</text></svg>

## O18 · Úloha jater v eliminaci léčiv, first-pass efekt

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 202" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="202" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">First-pass efekt — proč se stejná látka dávkuje ústy jinak než do žíly</text><rect x="30" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="107" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">100 % polknuté</text><text x="107" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">dávky</text><line x1="192" y1="101" x2="250" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="258" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="336" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">STŘEVNÍ STĚNA</text><text x="336" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">první ztráta</text><line x1="421" y1="101" x2="479" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="450" y="92" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">portální oběh</text><rect x="487" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="564" y="88" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">JÁTRA přes</text><text x="564" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">portální žílu ⚠️</text><text x="564" y="122" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">hlavní ztráta</text><line x1="650" y1="101" x2="708" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="678" y="92" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">FIRST-PASS</text><rect x="716" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="793" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">do těla dorazí</text><text x="793" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">třeba jen 10 %</text><text x="450" y="166" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">Obcházejí ho: sublingválně, i.v., i.m., transdermálně a částečně rektálně. Proto se nitroglycerin dává pod jazyk a testosteron</text><text x="450" y="181" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">injekčně.</text></svg>

## O19 · Inhibice a indukce enzymů léčivy, klinický význam

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 240" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="240" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Dva směry jedné interakce a jejich různá rychlost</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="141" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">INHIBITOR — brzda</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Makrolidy, azoly, ⚠️ grapefruit, ritonavir</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Enzym přestane pracovat HNED</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• → hladina druhého léku STOUPNE</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ hrozí PŘEDÁVKOVÁNÍ</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="141" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">INDUKTOR — plyn</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Rifampicin, karbamazepin, fenytoin, ⚠️ třezalka,</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">kouření</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Enzymu se musí NAROBIT — trvá dny</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• → hladina druhého léku KLESNE</text><text x="479" y="168" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ hrozí SELHÁNÍ LÉČBY (antikoncepce!)</text><text x="450" y="219" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Nebezpečí indukce nekončí vysazením induktoru — enzymy se ještě týdny odbourávají a hladina druhého léku pak vyskočí nahoru.</text></svg>

## O20 · Vylučování léčiv renální a extrarenální

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 202" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="202" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Tři děje v ledvině — a proč alkalizace moči urychlí vyloučení aspirinu</text><rect x="30" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="107" y="88" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">GLOMERULÁRNÍ</text><text x="107" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">FILTRACE ⚠️ jen</text><text x="107" y="122" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">VOLNÁ frakce</text><line x1="192" y1="101" x2="250" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="258" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="336" y="88" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">TUBULÁRNÍ SEKRECE</text><text x="336" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">aktivní, i vázané</text><text x="336" y="122" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">léky</text><line x1="421" y1="101" x2="479" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="487" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="564" y="88" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">TUBULÁRNÍ RESORPCE</text><text x="564" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ nenabité se</text><text x="564" y="122" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">vrací zpět</text><line x1="650" y1="101" x2="708" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="716" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="793" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">MOČ</text><text x="450" y="166" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">Zásaditá moč udrží kyselý lék v nabité formě → nevstřebá se zpět → odejde. Extrarenálně: žlučí (⚠️ enterohepatální oběh),</text><text x="450" y="181" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">plícemi, mlékem, slinami, potem.</text></svg>

## O21 · Účinek léčiv obecně, způsob účinku na molekulární úrovni

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 239" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="239" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Dva způsoby, jak léčivo vůbec může působit</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">SPECIFICKÝ účinek</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Přes konkrétní cílovou strukturu</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Receptor, enzym, kanál, přenašeč</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Působí v malých dávkách</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Většina léčiv</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#F4F7F5" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#C6D5D0" text-anchor="middle">NESPECIFICKÝ účinek</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Fyzikálně-chemický, bez receptoru</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Antacida, projímadla, dezinficiencia,</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• aktivní uhlí, osmotická diuretika</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Potřebuje velké dávky</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">Podle výsledku: kauzální (odstraní příčinu — antibiotikum) × symptomatická (uleví — analgetikum) × substituční (nahradí, co</text><text x="450" y="218" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">chybí — inzulin).</text></svg>

---

## O22 · Specifický účinek, receptorová teorie, typy receptorů

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 399" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="399" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Typ receptoru určuje, za jak dlouho lék zabere</text><rect x="96" y="56" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="80" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="start">IONOTROPNÍ = sám je kanál</text><text x="110" y="101" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">nikotinový, GABA-A, NMDA · ⚠️ MILISEKUNDY</text><rect x="96" y="134" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="158" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">SPŘAŽENÝ S G-PROTEINEM</text><text x="110" y="179" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">muskarinový, adrenergní, opioidní · sekundy</text><rect x="96" y="212" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="236" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">S VLASTNÍ ENZYMOVOU AKTIVITOU</text><text x="110" y="257" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">inzulinový receptor · minuty</text><rect x="96" y="290" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="314" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">NITROBUNĚČNÝ — mění přepis genů</text><text x="110" y="335" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">kortikoidy, hormony štítnice · ⚠️ HODINY</text><line x1="58" y1="62" x2="58" y2="352" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="30" y="206" font-size="12" fill="#556964" transform="rotate(-90 30 206)" text-anchor="middle">čím níž, tím pomaleji</text><text x="450" y="378" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">Proto adrenalin u anafylaxe zabere za minutu a kortikoid až za hodiny — a proto kortikoid anafylaxi sám nezachrání.</text></svg>

## O23 · Dávka a účinek, terapeutický index, terapeutické okno, NNT

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 224" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="224" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Terapeutické okno — jediné, co určuje, jestli se lék monitoruje</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">ŠIROKÉ okno</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Penicilin, většina běžných léků</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Mezi účinnou a toxickou dávkou je prostor</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Hladiny se neměří</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Bezpečné i při chybě v dávce</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ ÚZKÉ okno</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Digoxin, lithium, warfarin, theofylin,</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• fenytoin, aminoglykosidy, cytostatika</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ účinná a toxická dávka skoro splývají</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ MĚŘÍ SE HLADINY</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">NNT = kolik pacientů musíš léčit, aby jeden měl prospěch. Čím nižší, tím lepší lék.</text></svg>

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 288" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, kinetiku</text><line x1="258" y1="87" x2="322" y2="136" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="26" y="132" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" ## O25 · Lékové interakce

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 224" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="224" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Dvě zcela různé cesty, jak si dva léky vjedou do vlasů</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">FARMAKOKINETICKÉ</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Mění HLADINU druhého léku</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Vstřebání (antacida × tetracykliny)</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Vytěsnění z bílkoviny</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Indukce a inhibice enzymů</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">FARMAKODYNAMICKÉ</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Hladina je stejná, mění se ÚČINEK</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Sčítání (alkohol + benzodiazepin)</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Rušení (naloxon × morfin)</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ „Triple whammy" na ledviny</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">Aditivní 1+1=2 · synergie 1+1=5 · potenciace 0+1=5 (látka sama neúčinná zesílí druhou) · antagonismus = princip všech antidot.</text></svg>

## O26 · Farmakogenetika, genetický polymorfismus

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 368" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="368" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Proč jeden pacient nemá účinek a druhý je otrávený</text><rect x="30" y="137" width="250" height="92" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="155" y="187" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">STEJNÁ DÁVKA STEJNÉHO LÉKU</text><line x1="286" y1="183" x2="462" y2="93" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="129" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">POMALÝ metabolizátor</text><rect x="470" y="56" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ lék se hromadí → toxicita</text><line x1="286" y1="183" x2="462" y2="183" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="174" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">NORMÁLNÍ metabolizátor</text><rect x="470" y="146" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="187" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">očekávaný účinek</text><line x1="286" y1="183" x2="462" y2="273" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="219" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">ULTRARYCHLÝ metabolizátor</text><rect x="470" y="236" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="277" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ lék zmizí dřív, než zabere</text><text x="450" y="332" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">Příklady: CYP2D6 a KODEIN (proléčivo — ultrarychlí udělají moc morfinu, u dětí popsána úmrtí) · CYP2C19 a klopidogrel · TPMT a</text><text x="450" y="347" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">azathioprin · plazmatická cholinesteráza a sukcinylcholin · HLA-B*5701 a abakavir.</text></svg>
