## O27 · Tolerance, tachyfylaxe, rezistence

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 239" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="239" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Otupí se pacient, nebo bakterie? To je celý rozdíl</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">TOLERANCE / TACHYFYLAXE</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Slábne odpověď ORGANISMU</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Tolerance = pomalu, po dnech a týdnech</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Tachyfylaxe = během hodin</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Vyčerpané zásoby nebo down-regulace receptorů</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">REZISTENCE</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Změnil se PATOGEN nebo NÁDOR</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Betalaktamázy, změněný cíl,</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• efluxní pumpy, nižší propustnost</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Roste používáním antibiotik</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">Down-regulace = receptorů ubývá při nadbytku podnětu. Up-regulace = přibývá při blokádě → proto se betablokátor nikdy nevysazuje</text><text x="450" y="218" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">náhle.</text></svg>

---

## O28 · Vliv průvodních onemocnění, polypragmazie

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 368" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="368" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Proč každý přidaný lék není jen „+1"</text><rect x="30" y="137" width="250" height="92" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="155" y="179" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">POLYPRAGMAZIE 5 a více léků</text><text x="155" y="195" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">současně</text><line x1="286" y1="183" x2="462" y2="93" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="129" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">interakce rostou GEOMETRICKY</text><rect x="470" y="56" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">u 8 léků už je nikdo neuhlídá</text><line x1="286" y1="183" x2="462" y2="183" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="174" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">PRESKRIPČNÍ KASKÁDA</text><rect x="470" y="146" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="187" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">nežádoucí účinek se léčí dalším lékem</text><line x1="286" y1="183" x2="462" y2="273" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="219" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">horší adherence</text><rect x="470" y="236" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="277" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">pacient přestane brát i to důležité</text><text x="450" y="332" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Játra: nižší metabolismus a málo albuminu. Ledviny: kumulace. Srdeční selhání: horší prokrvení střeva i jater. Nemoc mění</text><text x="450" y="347" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">kinetiku stejně jako věk.</text></svg>

---

## O29 · Nežádoucí účinky léčiv

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 224" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="224" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Dva typy, které se řeší úplně jinak</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#F4F7F5" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#C6D5D0" text-anchor="middle">TYP A — „Augmented"</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Vyplývá z mechanismu léku</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ ZÁVISLÝ NA DÁVCE, předvídatelný</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Častý, obvykle méně závažný</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Krvácení po warfarinu, sucho po atropinu</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">TYP B — „Bizarre"</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• S mechanismem nesouvisí</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ NEZÁVISLÝ NA DÁVCE, nepředvídatelný</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Vzácný, ale často závažný</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Alergie, agranulocytóza, maligní hypertermie</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">Typ A se řeší snížením dávky. ⚠️ Typ B jen okamžitým vysazením a lék se nesmí podat znovu.</text></svg>

---

## O30 · Léková alergie, idiosynkrazie

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 239" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="239" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Ne každá „reakce na lék" je alergie</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">ALERGIE</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Zapojený IMUNITNÍ systém</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Nutná předchozí SENZIBILIZACE</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Při prvním podání nevznikne</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Vyrážka → anafylaxe; ⚠️ i malá dávka stačí</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">IDIOSYNKRAZIE</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Imunita v tom NENÍ</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Geneticky daná odchylka enzymu</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Může přijít hned napoprvé</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Hemolýza při deficitu G6PD</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Anafylaxe = adrenalin i.m., ne antihistaminikum. Anafylaktoidní reakce (kontrastní látka, vankomycin) vypadá stejně, ale</text><text x="450" y="218" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">imunitu nezapojuje.</text></svg>

---

## O31 · Karcinogenní a mutagenní účinky

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 202" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="202" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Proč se karcinogenita neprojeví v klinické studii</text><rect x="30" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="107" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">MUTAGEN poškodí</text><text x="107" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">DNA</text><line x1="192" y1="101" x2="250" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="258" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="336" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">mutace v klíčovém</text><text x="336" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">genu</text><line x1="421" y1="101" x2="479" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="487" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="564" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">ztráta kontroly</text><text x="564" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">dělení</text><line x1="650" y1="101" x2="708" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="716" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="793" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ NÁDOR — po</text><text x="793" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">LETECH</text><text x="450" y="166" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ U karcinogenů se předpokládá, že bezpečná dávka neexistuje. Testuje se AMESOVÝM TESTEM (mutagenita na bakteriích) a</text><text x="450" y="181" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">dlouhodobě na zvířatech; klasifikuje IARC.</text></svg>

---

## O32 · Léčiva v těhotenství, teratogenní účinek, léčiva v době kojení

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 336" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="336" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Kdy je plod nejzranitelnější</text><rect x="96" y="56" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="80" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#556964" text-anchor="start">1.–2. týden — „vše nebo nic"</text><text x="110" y="101" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">buď potrat, nebo se nic nestane</text><rect x="96" y="134" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="158" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">⚠️ 3.–8. TÝDEN — ORGANOGENEZE</text><text x="110" y="179" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">⚠️ NEJVĚTŠÍ RIZIKO VROZENÝCH VAD</text><rect x="96" y="212" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="236" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">9. týden až porod — růst a zrání</text><text x="110" y="257" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">spíš funkční poruchy a poruchy růstu</text><line x1="58" y1="62" x2="58" y2="274" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="30" y="167" font-size="12" fill="#556964" transform="rotate(-90 30 167)" text-anchor="middle">průběh těhotenství</text><text x="450" y="300" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Klasické teratogeny: isotretinoin, valproát, warfarin, ACE inhibitory a sartany, methotrexát, thalidomid, tetracykliny,</text><text x="450" y="315" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">lithium. ⚠️ Nejrizikovější doba je ta, kdy žena o těhotenství ještě neví.</text></svg>

---

## O33 · Farmakoterapie v dětství

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 239" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="239" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Dítě není malý dospělý — první fáze funguje, druhá ne</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">FÁZE I (oxidace)</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• U novorozence ČÁSTEČNĚ funguje</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Dozrává během prvních měsíců</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">•</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ FÁZE II (glukuronidace)</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ U novorozence NEFUNGUJE</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Dozrává až kolem 2 let</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ CHLORAMFENIKOL → GRAY BABY</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Sulfonamid → jádrový ikterus</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Reyeův syndrom: aspirin + dítě + viróza → jaterní encefalopatie. ⚠️ Tetracykliny barví zuby. Dávka se počítá na POVRCH těla,</text><text x="450" y="218" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">ne na hmotnost.</text></svg>

---

## O34 · Farmakoterapie ve stáří, polypragmazie

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 239" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="239" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Distribuce se ve stáří mění dvěma protichůdnými směry najednou</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">UBÝVÁ VODY</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Hydrofilní lék se má kam MÉNĚ rozředit</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• → ⚠️ KONCENTRACE hned stoupne</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Digoxin, aspirin, lithium</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Problém už po první dávce</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">PŘIBÝVÁ TUKU</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Lipofilní lék má kam se schovat</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• → ⚠️ větší Vd → DELŠÍ POLOČAS</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Benzodiazepiny</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Problém až za týden</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Normální kreatinin u seniora neznamená normální ledviny — vyrábí ho méně (úbytek svalů) A hůř ho vylučuje; ty změny se</text><text x="450" y="218" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">navzájem vyruší.</text></svg>

---

## O35 · Biologická léčba: rozdělení, názvosloví, biosimilars

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 414" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="414" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Názvosloví protilátek kopíruje historii oboru</text><rect x="96" y="56" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="80" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">-O- MYŠÍ protilátka</text><text x="110" y="101" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">nejvíc cizí → ⚠️ nejvíc imunogenní</text><rect x="96" y="134" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="158" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">-XI- CHIMÉRICKÁ</text><text x="110" y="179" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">infliximab, rituximab</text><rect x="96" y="212" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="236" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">-ZU- HUMANIZOVANÁ</text><text x="110" y="257" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">trastuzumab, omalizumab</text><rect x="96" y="290" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="314" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="start">-U- PLNĚ HUMÁNNÍ</text><text x="110" y="335" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">adalimumab, denosumab → nejméně imunogenní</text><line x1="58" y1="62" x2="58" y2="352" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="30" y="206" font-size="12" fill="#556964" transform="rotate(-90 30 206)" text-anchor="middle">čím níž, tím lidštější</text><text x="450" y="378" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Biosimilar není generikum — živá buňka nikdy nevyrobí dvě identické molekuly, liší se i dvě šarže originálu. ⚠️ Koncovka</text><text x="450" y="393" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">-tinib (imatinib) NENÍ biologikum, je to malá molekula.</text></svg>

---

## 36 · Cholinergní přenos vzruchu

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 202" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" — noradrenalin se vychytá zpět</text><rect x="30" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="107" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">cholin +</text><text x="107" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">acetyl-CoA</text><line x1="192" y1="101" x2="250" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="222" y="78" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">cholinacetyl-</text><text x="222" y="93" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">transferáza</text><rect x="258" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="336" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">ACETYLCHOLIN ve</text><text x="336" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">vezikule</text><line x1="421" y1="101" x2="479" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="450" y="78" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">⚠️ botulotoxin</text><text x="450" y="93" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">blokuje</text><rect x="487" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="564" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">EXOCYTÓZA do</text><text x="564" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">štěrbiny</text><line x1="650" y1="101" x2="708" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="678" y="78" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">receptory M1–M5,</text><text x="678" y="93" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">Nm, Nn</text><rect x="716" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="793" y="88" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ ROZŠTĚPÍ HO</text><text x="793" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">ENZYM</text><text x="793" y="122" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">acetylcholinesteráza</text><text x="450" y="166" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">Proto na acetylcholin fungují inhibitory esterázy a na noradrenalin blokátory reuptake. ⚠️ M2 = srdce (jediný tlumivý) · M3 =</text><text x="450" y="181" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">žlázy a hladký sval · Nm = ploténka.</text></svg>

x="450" y="184" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">acetylcholinu</text><rect x="26" y="56" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">na xerostomii</text><line x1="258" y1="87" x2="322" y2="136" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="26" y="132" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="167" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">MIÓZA — léčba glaukomu</text><line x1="258" y1="163" x2="322" y2="163" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="26" y="208" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="243" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">BRADYKARDIE</text><line x1="258" y1="239" x2="322" y2="190" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="56" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="83" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ SEKRECE KYSELINY — KI u</text><text x="761" y="175" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" ## 38 · Nepřímá cholinomimetika

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 239" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="239" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Jedno písmeno rozhoduje, jestli lék projde do mozku</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">NEOSTIGMIN — KVARTÉRNÍ</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Nabitý → ⚠️ NEPROJDE do mozku</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Myasthenia gravis, dekurarizace</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Podává se s ATROPINEM</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• aby nezpůsobil bradykardii</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">FYZOSTIGMIN — TERCIÁRNÍ</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ PROJDE do mozku</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• → antidotum centrálního</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• anticholinergního syndromu</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• (durman, atropin, TCA)</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Otrava organofosfáty: atropin (na M receptory) + PRALIDOXIM (reaktivátor enzymu) — ten musí přijít dřív, než enzym</text><text x="450" y="218" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">„zestárne".</text></svg>

## 39 · Parasympatolytika

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 328" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="328" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Pět přirovnání, ze kterých poznáš otravu atropinem</text><rect x="328" y="101" width="244" height="124" rx="14" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2"/><text x="450" y="158" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">ATROPIN blokáda M receptorů =</text><text x="450" y="175" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">obrácený parasympatikus</text><rect x="26" y="56" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="83" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">„slepý jako netopýr"</text><text x="139" y="99" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">mydriáza, cykloplegie</text><line x1="258" y1="87" x2="322" y2="136" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="26" y="132" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="159" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ „suchý jako kost"</text><text x="139" y="175" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">XEROSTOMIE → KAZ</text><line x1="258" y1="163" x2="322" y2="163" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="26" y="208" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="243" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">„červený jako řepa"</text><line x1="258" y1="239" x2="322" y2="190" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="56" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="83" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">„horký jako pec" nepotí se,</text><text x="761" y="99" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">nemá jak chladit</text><line x1="642" y1="87" x2="578" y2="136" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="132" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="159" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">„šílený jako kloboučník"</text><text x="761" y="175" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">delirium</text><line x1="642" y1="163" x2="578" y2="163" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="208" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="235" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">tachykardie, zácpa, retence</text><text x="761" y="251" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">moči</text><line x1="642" y1="239" x2="578" y2="190" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="450" y="292" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Zubařsky: xerostomii nedělá jen atropin — stejně suší tricyklická antidepresiva, antipsychotika, antihistaminika a léky na</text><text x="450" y="307" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">hyperaktivní měchýř. A ty pacient bere roky.</text></svg>

## 40 · Adrenergní přenos vzruchu

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 202" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="202" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Syntéza katecholaminů a kde vzniká adrenalin</text><rect x="30" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="107" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">TYROSIN</text><line x1="192" y1="101" x2="250" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="222" y="78" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">tyrosin-</text><text x="222" y="93" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">hydroxyláza</text><rect x="258" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="336" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">DOPA ⚠️ krok</text><text x="336" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">určující rychlost</text><line x1="421" y1="101" x2="479" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="450" y="92" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">dekarboxyláza</text><rect x="487" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="564" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">DOPAMIN</text><line x1="650" y1="101" x2="708" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="678" y="78" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">dopamin-β-</text><text x="678" y="93" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">hydroxyláza</text><rect x="716" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="793" y="88" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">NORADRENALIN ⚠️ v</text><text x="793" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">nadledvině →</text><text x="793" y="122" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">ADRENALIN</text><text x="450" y="166" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Zánik NENÍ enzymem ve štěrbině, ale REUPTAKE zpět do neuronu (kokain a tricyklika ho blokují); teprve pak MAO uvnitř a COMT</text><text x="450" y="181" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">vně. Adrenalin vzniká jen v dřeni nadledvin — jen tam je enzym PNMT.</text></svg>

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 313" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" text-anchor="middle">ADRENALIN u ANAFYLAXE ⚠️ i.m.</text><text x="450" y="175" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">do stehna</text><rect x="26" y="56" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="83" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">α1 — stáhne cévy → zvedne</text><text x="139" y="99" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">TLAK</text><line x1="258" y1="87" x2="322" y2="136" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="26" y="132" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="167" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">β1 — podpoří SRDCE</text><line x1="258" y1="163" x2="322" y2="163" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="26" y="208" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="243" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">β2 — rozšíří PRŮDUŠKY</text><line x1="258" y1="239" x2="322" y2="190" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="56" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="83" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">β2 — zastaví výdej mediátorů</text><text x="761" y="99" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">ze žírných buněk</text><line x1="642" y1="87" x2="578" y2="136" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="132" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="159" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ antihistaminikum tohle</text><text x="761" y="175" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">NEUMÍ</text><line x1="642" y1="163" x2="578" y2="163" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="208" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="235" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ kortikoid působí až za</text><text x="761" y="251" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">hodiny</text><line x1="642" y1="239" x2="578" y2="190" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="450" y="292" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">DOPAMIN má tři chování podle dávky: nízká → D1 ledviny · střední → β1 srdce · vysoká → α1 stah cév.</text></svg>

---

## 42 · Sympatomimetika alfa

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 224" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="224" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Paradox: obě jsou „alfa", ale s tlakem dělají opak</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">α1 — na periferii</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Fenylefrin, midodrin, xylometazolin</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Stah cév → ⚠️ ZVYŠUJE TLAK</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Dekongesce nosu, mydriáza bez cykloplegie</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Nosní kapky max 7 dní</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">α2 — CENTRÁLNĚ, PŘED synapsí</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Klonidin, methyldopa, brimonidin</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Sedne si na BRZDU výdeje sympatiku</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• → ⚠️ SNIŽUJE TLAK</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Methyldopa = volba v graviditě</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Rhinitis medicamentosa: po odeznění kapek sliznice oteče ještě víc, pacient kápne znovu a je v kruhu.</text></svg>

---

## 43 · Sympatomimetika beta

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 199" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="199" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Tři nežádoucí účinky β2-mimetik plynou z jediného receptoru</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">CO CHCEME — β2 v průduškách</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• SABA salbutamol, fenoterol — úlevově</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• LABA formoterol, salmeterol</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ LABA NIKDY samotná bez kortikoidu</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Tokolytika: hexoprenalin</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">CO PŘIJDE S TÍM — β2 jinde</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ TREMOR (β2 na kosterním svalu)</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ TACHYKARDIE (přelití na β1)</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ HYPOKALEMIE (β2 žene K⁺ do buňky)</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• → využito u akutní hyperkalemie</text></svg>

---

## 44 · Nepřímá sympatomimetika

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 202" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="202" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Potřebují mít z čeho brát — proto jim rychle dojde dech</text><rect x="30" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="107" y="88" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">efedrin,</text><text x="107" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">amfetamin, ⚠️</text><text x="107" y="122" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">TYRAMIN</text><line x1="192" y1="101" x2="250" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="258" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="336" y="88" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">vytlačí</text><text x="336" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">NORADRENALIN z</text><text x="336" y="122" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">vezikul</text><line x1="421" y1="101" x2="479" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="487" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="564" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">silná sympatická</text><text x="564" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">odpověď</text><line x1="650" y1="101" x2="708" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="716" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="793" y="88" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ vezikuly jsou</text><text x="793" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">prázdné →</text><text x="793" y="122" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">TACHYFYLAXE</text><text x="450" y="166" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Sýrový efekt: tyramin z uzrálých sýrů normálně zničí střevní MAO. U pacienta na neselektivním inhibitoru MAO projde, vytlačí</text><text x="450" y="181" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">noradrenalin → HYPERTENZNÍ KRIZE. Kokain naopak blokuje reuptake.</text></svg>

## 45 · Sympatolytika alfa

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 202" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="202" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Pořadí, které se ptají skoro vždycky</text><rect x="30" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="107" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">FEOCHROMOCYTOM</text><line x1="192" y1="101" x2="250" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="258" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="336" y="80" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ NEJDŘÍV</text><text x="336" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">α-BLOKÁDA</text><text x="336" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">fentolamin,</text><text x="336" y="130" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">fenoxybenzamin</text><line x1="421" y1="101" x2="479" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="487" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="564" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">teprve POTOM</text><text x="564" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">betablokátor</text><line x1="650" y1="101" x2="708" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="716" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="793" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">bezpečná operace</text><text x="450" y="166" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Obráceně: zablokuješ β2 vazodilataci, zůstane čistý α1 stah → hypertenzní krize. ⚠️ Tamsulosin je selektivní na α1A v</text><text x="450" y="181" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">prostatě → nesnižuje tlak, ale způsobuje „floppy iris" při operaci šedého zákalu.</text></svg>

## 46 · Sympatolytika beta (betablokátory)

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 239" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="239" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Selektivita je celý rozdíl mezi účinkem a nežádoucím účinkem</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">β1 — SRDCE = žádaný účinek</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Nižší tep a slabší stah</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Méně reninu → nižší tlak</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ICHS, hypertenze, arytmie</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Srdeční selhání — jen 4 z nich, pomalu titrovat</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">β2 — PLÍCE a jinde = nežádoucí</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ BRONCHOSPAZMUS — KI u astmatu</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Maskuje hypoglykemii (pocení zůstane)</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Studené končetiny, únava</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Nikdy nevysazovat náhle — rebound</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">Neselektivní: propranolol, sotalol, ⚠️ timolol (i v očních kapkách!). β1-selektivní: metoprolol, bisoprolol, atenolol. S</text><text x="450" y="218" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">vazodilatací: karvedilol, nebivolol.</text></svg>

---

## 47 · Myorelaxancia

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 239" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="239" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Proč u jednoho antidotum je a u druhého ne</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">SUKCINYLCHOLIN — depolarizující</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Nejdřív fascikulace, pak ochabnutí</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ NEMÁ ANTIDOTUM</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Hyperkalemie, maligní hypertermie</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Atypická pseudocholinesteráza → apnoe</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">ROKURONIUM ap. — nedepolarizující</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Kompetitivní blokáda ploténky</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Antidotum NEOSTIGMIN + atropin</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ U rokuronia SUGAMMADEX (obalí ho)</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Atrakurium: ⚠️ Hofmannova eliminace</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ U depolarizujícího bloku není problém nedostatek acetylcholinu, ale jeho nadbytek — neostigmin by blok prohloubil. Dantrolen</text><text x="450" y="218" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">působí přímo na sval (sarkoplazmatické retikulum) — maligní hypertermie.</text></svg>

---

## 48 · Lokální anestetika

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 217" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="217" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Musí projít dovnitř, aby zavřelo kanál zevnitř</text><rect x="30" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="107" y="88" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">ANESTETIKUM =</text><text x="107" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">slabá zásada</text><text x="107" y="122" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">nenabité</text><line x1="192" y1="101" x2="250" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="258" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="336" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">projde membránou</text><line x1="421" y1="101" x2="479" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="487" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="564" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">uvnitř se nabije</text><line x1="650" y1="101" x2="708" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="716" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="793" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">zavře Na⁺ kanál</text><text x="793" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">ZEVNITŘ</text><text x="450" y="166" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ ZÁNĚT = kyselé pH → skoro všechno je nabité UŽ VENKU → dovnitř se nedostane → V ZANÍCENÉM ZUBU ANESTEZIE NEZABERE. Estery</text><text x="450" y="181" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">(prokain) → alergie na PABA. Amidy (lidokain, ⚠️ ARTIKAIN) → játra. ⚠️ Bupivakain je kardiotoxický, prilokain dělá</text><text x="450" y="196" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">methemoglobinemii. Antidotum toxicity: LIPIDOVÁ EMULZE.</text></svg>

---

## 49 · Celková anestetika — inhalační

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 239" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="239" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Dvě veličiny, které spolu vůbec nesouvisí</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">MAC = jak je SILNÉ</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Minimální alveolární koncentrace</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ NÍZKÁ MAC = SILNÉ anestetikum</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Oxid dusný má MAC &gt; 100 %</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• → sám nikdy neuspí</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">ROZPUSTNOST V KRVI = jak RYCHLE</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Koeficient krev/plyn</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ NÍZKÁ rozpustnost = RYCHLÝ nástup</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• co se nerozpustí v krvi, nasytí mozek</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Desfluran nejrychlejší, sevofluran standard</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Oxid dusný: výborná analgezie → sedace u dětí a úzkostných pacientů v zubní ordinaci; rizika difuzní hypoxie a inaktivace</text><text x="450" y="218" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">vitaminu B12. ⚠️ Maligní hypertermie po halogenovaných → dantrolen.</text></svg>

---

## 50 · Celková anestetika — intravenózní

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 288" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="288" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Každé má jednu přednost a jednu nevýhodu — podle toho se vybírá pacient</text><rect x="328" y="101" width="244" height="124" rx="14" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2"/><text x="450" y="158" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">ČÍM UVEDU PACIENTA DO</text><text x="450" y="175" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">ANESTEZIE?</text><rect x="26" y="56" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="74" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">PROPOFOL rychlý,</text><text x="139" y="91" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">antiemetický ⚠️ sráží tlak,</text><text x="139" y="108" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">bez analgezie</text><line x1="258" y1="87" x2="322" y2="136" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="26" y="132" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="150" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">THIOPENTAL ⚠️ krátkost je</text><text x="139" y="167" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">REDISTRIBUCÍ, ne</text><text x="139" y="184" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">metabolismem → kumuluje</text><line x1="258" y1="163" x2="322" y2="163" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="26" y="208" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="226" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">KETAMIN ⚠️ jako jediný</text><text x="139" y="243" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">ZVYŠUJE tlak a nechá dýchat</text><text x="139" y="260" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">→ šok, terén</text><line x1="258" y1="239" x2="322" y2="190" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="56" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="74" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">ETOMIDÁT kardiálně</text><text x="761" y="91" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">nejstabilnější ⚠️ tlumí kůru</text><text x="761" y="108" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">nadledvin</text><line x1="642" y1="87" x2="578" y2="136" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="132" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="159" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">MIDAZOLAM anxiolýza +</text><text x="761" y="175" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">amnézie</text><line x1="642" y1="163" x2="578" y2="163" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="208" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="226" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Všechna kromě ketaminu</text><text x="761" y="243" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">jdou přes GABA-A; ketamin</text><text x="761" y="260" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">blokuje NMDA</text><line x1="642" y1="239" x2="578" y2="190" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/></svg>

## 51 · Hypnotika

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 224" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="224" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Proč barbiturát zabíjí a benzodiazepin (skoro) ne</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">BENZODIAZEPIN</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Zvyšuje FREKVENCI otevírání kanálu</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Bez vlastní GABA neudělá NIC</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• → ⚠️ MÁ STROP účinku</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Samo o sobě jen výjimečně zabije</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">BARBITURÁT</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Prodlužuje DOBU otevření</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Ve vyšší dávce otevře kanál I BEZ GABA</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• → ⚠️ NEMÁ STROP, nemá antidotum</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Proto se přestal používat na spaní</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">Z-hypnotika (zolpidem) míří na podjednotku α1 → uspí bez myorelaxace; ⚠️ parasomnie — noční jedení a chození s amnézií.</text></svg>

## 52 · Benzodiazepiny

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 328" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="328" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Pět účinků najednou — a nedají se od sebe oddělit</text><rect x="328" y="101" width="244" height="124" rx="14" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2"/><text x="450" y="158" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">BENZODIAZEPIN pozitivní</text><text x="450" y="175" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">modulátor GABA-A</text><rect x="26" y="56" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="91" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">ANXIOLÝZA</text><line x1="258" y1="87" x2="322" y2="136" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="26" y="132" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="167" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">SEDACE a HYPNÓZA</text><line x1="258" y1="163" x2="322" y2="163" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="26" y="208" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="235" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">ANTIKONVULZE — status</text><text x="139" y="251" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">epilepticus</text><line x1="258" y1="239" x2="322" y2="190" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="56" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="91" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">MYORELAXACE</text><line x1="642" y1="87" x2="578" y2="136" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="132" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="150" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ ANTEROGRÁDNÍ AMNÉZIE</text><text x="761" y="167" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">(proto midazolam před</text><text x="761" y="184" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">výkonem)</text><line x1="642" y1="163" x2="578" y2="163" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="208" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="235" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Tolerance, závislost,</text><text x="761" y="251" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">rebound</text><line x1="642" y1="239" x2="578" y2="190" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="450" y="292" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ LOT — lorazepam, oxazepam, temazepam — se jen konjugují (bez fáze I) → bezpečné u jater a u seniorů. Antidotum FLUMAZENIL, ⚠️</text><text x="450" y="307" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">opatrně: u závislého vyvolá křeče.</text></svg>

## 53 · Antiepileptika

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 458" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="458" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Čtyři mechanismy — z nich si zástupce odvodíš</text><rect x="30" y="182" width="250" height="92" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="155" y="224" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">JAK ZTIŠIT NADMĚRNÝ VÝBOJ</text><text x="155" y="240" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">NEURONŮ</text><line x1="286" y1="228" x2="462" y2="93" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="152" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">zavřít Na⁺ kanály</text><rect x="470" y="56" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">fenytoin · karbamazepin · lamotrigin · valproát</text><line x1="286" y1="228" x2="462" y2="183" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="196" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">zavřít T-Ca²⁺ kanály</text><rect x="470" y="146" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="187" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ ETHOSUXIMID — jen absence</text><line x1="286" y1="228" x2="462" y2="273" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="242" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">zesílit GABA</text><rect x="470" y="236" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="277" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">benzodiazepiny · barbituráty · vigabatrin</text><line x1="286" y1="228" x2="462" y2="363" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="286" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">tlumit glutamát / SV2A</text><rect x="470" y="326" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="367" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">topiramát · perampanel · levetiracetam</text><text x="450" y="422" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ FENYTOIN: HYPERPLAZIE GINGIVY (stejně jako cyklosporin a nifedipin) + nelineární kinetika. ⚠️ VALPROÁT je nejsilnější</text><text x="450" y="437" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">teratogen. ⚠️ KARBAMAZEPIN: autoindukce, hyponatremie, HLA-B*1502 → Stevensův–Johnsonův syndrom.</text></svg>
