## 54 · Antiparkinsonika

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 202" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="202" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Proč se levodopa nikdy nedává samotná</text><rect x="30" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="107" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">LEVODOPA polknutá</text><line x1="192" y1="101" x2="250" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="258" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="336" y="80" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ na PERIFERII se</text><text x="336" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">hned mění na</text><text x="336" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">dopamin → nauzea,</text><text x="336" y="130" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">hypotenze</text><line x1="421" y1="101" x2="479" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="487" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="564" y="88" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">+ KARBIDOPA /</text><text x="564" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">BENSERAZID ⚠️ ty</text><text x="564" y="122" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">do mozku NEPROJDOU</text><line x1="650" y1="101" x2="708" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="716" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="793" y="80" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">do mozku dorazí</text><text x="793" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">víc levodopy →</text><text x="793" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">dopamin TAM, kde</text><text x="793" y="130" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">chybí</text><text x="450" y="166" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Dopamin sám hematoencefalickou bariéru neprojde, levodopa ano. ⚠️ Parkinsonikovi se NESMÍ dát metoklopramid ani klasické</text><text x="450" y="181" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">antipsychotikum — blokují D2; bezpečný je domperidon.</text></svg>

---

## 55 · Neuroleptika (antipsychotika)

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 458" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="458" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Jedna blokáda je léčba, tři jsou nežádoucí účinky</text><rect x="30" y="182" width="250" height="92" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="155" y="224" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">BLOKÁDA RECEPTORŮ D2 lék neumí</text><text x="155" y="240" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">rozlišit dráhy</text><line x1="286" y1="228" x2="462" y2="93" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="152" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">MEZOLIMBICKÁ</text><rect x="470" y="56" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">⭐ ÚČINEK — halucinace a bludy ustoupí</text><line x1="286" y1="228" x2="462" y2="183" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="196" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">MEZOKORTIKÁLNÍ</text><rect x="470" y="146" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="187" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ ZHORŠÍ negativní příznaky a kognici</text><line x1="286" y1="228" x2="462" y2="273" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="242" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">NIGROSTRIATÁLNÍ</text><rect x="470" y="236" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="277" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ EXTRAPYRAMIDOVÉ nežádoucí účinky</text><line x1="286" y1="228" x2="462" y2="363" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="286" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">TUBEROINFUNDIBULÁRNÍ</text><rect x="470" y="326" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="367" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ HYPERPROLAKTINEMIE — dopamin je brzda prolaktinu</text><text x="450" y="422" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">🔑 V pořadí, jak přicházejí: hodiny → dystonie · dny → akatizie · týdny → parkinsonismus · ⚠️ měsíce až roky → TARDIVNÍ DYSKINEZE</text><text x="450" y="437" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">(často nevratná, v orofaciální oblasti). ⚠️ Klozapin = nejúčinnější u rezistence, ale agranulocytóza.</text></svg>

## 56 · Antidepresiva — tricyklická, inhibitory MAO

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 328" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="328" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Jeden žádaný účinek a čtyři nežádoucí ze čtyř dalších receptorů</text><rect x="328" y="101" width="244" height="124" rx="14" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2"/><text x="450" y="158" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">TRICYKLICKÉ ANTIDEPRESIVUM</text><text x="450" y="175" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">„špinavý" lék</text><rect x="26" y="56" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="83" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">blok reuptake NA a 5-HT ⭐</text><text x="139" y="99" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">tohle je žádaný účinek</text><line x1="258" y1="87" x2="322" y2="136" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="26" y="132" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="159" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ blok M → XEROSTOMIE →</text><text x="139" y="175" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">KAZ, zácpa, retence</text><line x1="258" y1="163" x2="322" y2="163" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="26" y="208" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="235" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ blok H1 → sedace,</text><text x="139" y="251" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">přírůstek hmotnosti</text><line x1="258" y1="239" x2="322" y2="190" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="56" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="91" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ blok α1 → ortostáza, pády</text><line x1="642" y1="87" x2="578" y2="136" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="132" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="159" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ blok Na⁺ v srdci →</text><text x="761" y="175" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">ARYTMIE = příčina smrti</text><line x1="642" y1="163" x2="578" y2="163" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="208" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="235" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ zásoba na 14 dní může být</text><text x="761" y="251" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">smrtelná dávka</text><line x1="642" y1="239" x2="578" y2="190" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="450" y="292" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ IMAO: sýrová reakce s tyraminem; s SSRI serotoninový syndrom. Rozlišení: serotoninový má HYPERreflexii a myoklonus,</text><text x="450" y="307" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">neuroleptický RIGIDITU.</text></svg>

## 57 · Antidepresiva — SSRI, SNRI, atypická

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 202" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="202" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Past latence — hybnost se upraví dřív než nálada</text><rect x="30" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="107" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">nasazení SSRI</text><line x1="192" y1="101" x2="250" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="258" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="336" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">1.–2. týden: ⚠️</text><text x="336" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">vrátí se ENERGIE</text><line x1="421" y1="101" x2="479" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="487" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="564" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">2.–4. týden:</text><text x="564" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">zlepší se NÁLADA</text><line x1="650" y1="101" x2="708" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="716" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="793" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">plný účinek</text><text x="450" y="166" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Proto na začátku léčby STOUPÁ riziko sebevraždy: pacient už má sílu jednat, ale ještě má depresivní myšlenky. ⚠️ Zubařsky:</text><text x="450" y="181" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">SSRI vyprázdní serotonin z trombocytů → DELŠÍ KRVÁCENÍ PO EXTRAKCI, zvlášť s NSA.</text></svg>

## 58 · Anxiolytika, stabilizátory nálady

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 328" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="328" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Nejužší terapeutické okno v psychiatrii</text><rect x="328" y="101" width="244" height="124" rx="14" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2"/><text x="450" y="158" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">LITHIUM ⚠️ terapeutické</text><text x="450" y="175" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">rozmezí 0,6–1,2 mmol/l</text><rect x="26" y="56" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="83" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Vylučuje se VÝHRADNĚ</text><text x="139" y="99" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">LEDVINAMI</text><line x1="258" y1="87" x2="322" y2="136" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="26" y="132" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="167" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ NSA zvednou hladinu</text><line x1="258" y1="163" x2="322" y2="163" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="26" y="208" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="235" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ ACE inhibitory a sartany</text><text x="139" y="251" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">zvednou hladinu</text><line x1="258" y1="239" x2="322" y2="190" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="56" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="83" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Thiazidy a dehydratace</text><text x="761" y="99" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">zvednou hladinu</text><line x1="642" y1="87" x2="578" y2="136" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="132" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="159" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">NÚ: třes, polyurie,</text><text x="761" y="175" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">hypotyreóza</text><line x1="642" y1="163" x2="578" y2="163" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="208" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="235" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Teratogen — Ebsteinova</text><text x="761" y="251" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">anomálie</text><line x1="642" y1="239" x2="578" y2="190" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="450" y="292" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">Při nedostatku sodíku si ledvina lithium plete se sodíkem a šetří ho. Anxiolytika: BZD jen krátkodobě, dlouhodobě SSRI; buspiron</text><text x="450" y="307" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">nenávykový, ale pomalý.</text></svg>

## 59 · Farmakoterapie Alzheimerovy choroby, nootropika

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 224" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="224" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Léčba je přesný opak anticholinergik — a proto se nesmí kombinovat</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">INHIBITORY AChE</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Donepezil, rivastigmin, galantamin</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Lehké až středně těžké stadium</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ NÚ = SLUDGE nasvětlejší formě:</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• hypersalivace, průjem, bradykardie</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ CO NEDÁVAT</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Anticholinergika (oxybutynin!)</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• — ruší si tím vlastní léčbu</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Benzodiazepiny a klasická antipsychotika</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• zhorší kognici a zvyšují úmrtnost</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">Memantin (NMDA) u těžších stadií. ⚠️ Nic z toho nemoc nezastaví. Nootropika (piracetam, ginkgo) mají slabou až žádnou evidenci.</text></svg>

---

## 60 · Opium a jeho alkaloidy

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 239" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="239" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Co při dlouhodobém podávání povolí a co ne</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#F4F7F5" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#C6D5D0" text-anchor="middle">CO SE TOLERUJE</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Analgezie</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Euforie</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Útlum dechu</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Nauzea</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ CO SE NETOLERUJE NIKDY</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ MIÓZA — proto je diagnostická</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ ZÁCPA — proto se laxativum</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• nasazuje SOUČASNĚ s opioidem</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• a nikdy až potom</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Příčina smrti = útlum dechového centra (klesne citlivost k CO₂). Antidotum NALOXON má ⚠️ kratší poločas než morfin → musí se</text><text x="450" y="218" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">opakovat. Papaverin a noskapin (isochinolinové) analgezii ani závislost nedělají.</text></svg>

## 61 · Deriváty a náhražky morfinu

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 414" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="414" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Řazení podle síly a podle chování na receptoru</text><rect x="96" y="56" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="80" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="start">SILNÉ — plní agonisté μ</text><text x="110" y="101" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">fentanyl (100× morfin), sufentanil, oxykodon, metadon</text><rect x="96" y="134" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="158" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">PARCIÁLNÍ — buprenorfin</text><text x="110" y="179" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">⚠️ má strop dechového útlumu, ale naloxon ho těžko vytlačí</text><rect x="96" y="212" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="236" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">SLABÉ — kodein, tramadol</text><text x="110" y="257" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">⚠️ tramadol: + reuptake 5-HT/NA → serotoninový sy, křeče</text><rect x="96" y="290" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="314" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">ANTAGONISTÉ</text><text x="110" y="335" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">naloxon i.v. · naltrexon p.o. · ⚠️ methylnaltrexon jen na zácpu</text><line x1="58" y1="62" x2="58" y2="352" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="30" y="206" font-size="12" fill="#556964" transform="rotate(-90 30 206)" text-anchor="middle">klesající síla</text><text x="450" y="378" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Petidin → metabolit norpetidin dráždí CNS (křeče), smrtelná interakce s IMAO. ⚠️ Metadon prodlužuje QT. ⚠️ Fentanyl i.v.</text><text x="450" y="393" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">rychle → rigidita hrudní stěny.</text></svg>

## 62 · Eikosanoidy

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 278" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="278" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Dvě větve — a proč kortikoid funguje šířeji než NSA</text><rect x="30" y="92" width="250" height="92" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="155" y="125" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">KYSELINA ARACHIDONOVÁ ⚠️ o</text><text x="155" y="142" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">patro výš ji blokují KORTIKOIDY</text><text x="155" y="159" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">(fosfolipáza A₂)</text><line x1="286" y1="138" x2="462" y2="93" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="106" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">COX — ⚠️ tady blokují NSA</text><rect x="470" y="56" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">prostaglandiny · prostacyklin · tromboxan</text><line x1="286" y1="138" x2="462" y2="183" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="152" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">LOX — ⚠️ NSA sem NESAHAJÍ</text><rect x="470" y="146" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="187" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">LEUKOTRIENY — stah průdušek, hlen</text><text x="450" y="242" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Zavřeš COX → kyselina se PŘELIJE do větve leukotrienů → ASPIRINEM INDUKOVANÉ ASTMA (Samterova trias: astma + nosní polypy +</text><text x="450" y="257" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">intolerance aspirinu). COX-1 = žaludeční hlen, tromboxan, ledviny. COX-2 = zánět.</text></svg>

## 63 · Analgetika-antipyretika

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 217" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="217" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Celá otrava paracetamolem v jednom řádku</text><rect x="30" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="107" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">PARACETAMOL</text><line x1="192" y1="101" x2="250" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="258" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="336" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">90 % konjugace →</text><text x="336" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">neškodné</text><line x1="421" y1="101" x2="479" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="487" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="564" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">10 % CYP2E1 → ⚠️</text><text x="564" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">NAPQI</text><line x1="650" y1="101" x2="708" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="716" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="793" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">GLUTATHION ⚠️</text><text x="793" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">dokud vydrží</text><text x="450" y="166" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Alkoholik má obojí proti sobě: indukovaný CYP2E1 (víc NAPQI) A vyčerpaný glutathion. ⚠️ Příznaky přijdou až za 1–3 dny —</text><text x="450" y="181" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">rozhoduje HLADINA V ČASE, ne stav pacienta. Antidotum N-ACETYLCYSTEIN. ⚠️ Po extrakci je ibuprofen účinnější než paracetamol;</text><text x="450" y="196" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">nejlepší je jejich kombinace.</text></svg>

---

## 64 · Nesteroidní antiflogistika

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 328" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="328" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Skoro všechny nežádoucí účinky plynou z blokády COX-1</text><rect x="328" y="101" width="244" height="124" rx="14" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2"/><text x="450" y="158" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">BLOKÁDA COX-1 tam, kde ji</text><text x="450" y="175" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">tělo POTŘEBUJE</text><rect x="26" y="56" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="83" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">ŽALUDEK — ⚠️ vřed a krvácení</text><text x="139" y="99" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">(prevence PPI)</text><line x1="258" y1="87" x2="322" y2="136" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="26" y="132" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="150" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">LEDVINA — ⚠️ zruší</text><text x="139" y="167" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">vazodilataci přívodné</text><text x="139" y="184" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">tepénky</text><line x1="258" y1="163" x2="322" y2="163" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="26" y="208" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="235" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">DESTIČKY — ⚠️ méně</text><text x="139" y="251" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">tromboxanu → krvácivost</text><line x1="258" y1="239" x2="322" y2="190" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="56" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="74" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Aspirin acetyluje</text><text x="761" y="91" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">IREVERZIBILNĚ → účinek 7–10</text><text x="761" y="108" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">dní</text><line x1="642" y1="87" x2="578" y2="136" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="132" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="159" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Reyeův syndrom u dětí s</text><text x="761" y="175" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">virózou</text><line x1="642" y1="163" x2="578" y2="163" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="208" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="235" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Koxiby šetří žaludek, ale</text><text x="761" y="251" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">zvyšují KV riziko</text><line x1="642" y1="239" x2="578" y2="190" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="450" y="292" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ „Triple whammy": NSA + ACE inhibitor + diuretikum = akutní selhání ledvin. ⚠️ Ibuprofen ruší antiagregační účinek aspirinu —</text><text x="450" y="307" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">obsadí COX-1 dřív.</text></svg>

## 65 · Farmakoterapie migrény

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 239" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="239" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Dvě zcela oddělené léčby, které se nesmí zaměnit</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">AKUTNĚ — hned na začátku záchvatu</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• NSA nebo paracetamol</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ + METOKLOPRAMID — nejen proti nevolnosti,</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ale aby se analgetikum vůbec vstřebalo</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• TRIPTANY (5-HT1B/1D) ⚠️ KI u ICHS</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">PROFYLAXE — při 4+ záchvatech měsíčně</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Betablokátory, topiramát, amitriptylin</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Kandesartan, flunarizin, valproát</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Protilátky proti CGRP</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Hodnotí se až po 2–3 měsících</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Analgetika víc než 10–15 dní v měsíci → bolest hlavy Z NADUŽÍVÁNÍ LÉKŮ. Řešením je vysazení, ne přidání. ⚠️ Migréna s aurou =</text><text x="450" y="218" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">KI kombinované antikoncepce.</text></svg>

---

## 66 · Léčiva s pozitivně inotropním účinkem, digoxin

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 202" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="202" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Proč blokáda sodíkové pumpy posílí stah srdce</text><rect x="30" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="107" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">DIGOXIN zablokuje</text><text x="107" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">Na⁺/K⁺-ATPázu</text><line x1="192" y1="101" x2="250" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="258" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="336" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">sodíku uvnitř</text><text x="336" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">PŘIBUDE</text><line x1="421" y1="101" x2="479" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="487" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="564" y="88" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">výměník Na⁺/Ca²⁺</text><text x="564" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">přestane vyvážet</text><text x="564" y="122" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">vápník</text><line x1="650" y1="101" x2="708" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="716" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="793" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ VÍC VÁPNÍKU →</text><text x="793" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">SILNĚJŠÍ STAH</text><text x="450" y="166" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ HYPOKALEMIE ZVYŠUJE TOXICITU — draslík a digoxin soutěží o stejné místo na pumpě. A hypokalemii dělají diuretika, která má</text><text x="450" y="181" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">srdeční pacient skoro vždy. Otrava: nauzea · ⚠️ ŽLUTÉ VIDĚNÍ · arytmie. Antidotum: protilátky (Fab).</text></svg>

---

## 67 · Antiarytmika

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 492" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="492" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Vaughanova–Williamsova klasifikace podle blokovaného kanálu</text><rect x="96" y="56" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="80" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">I. — blokátory Na⁺ kanálů</text><text x="110" y="101" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">IA chinidin · IB LIDOKAIN (komorové) · ⚠️ IC propafenon</text><rect x="96" y="134" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="158" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="start">II. — BETABLOKÁTORY</text><text x="110" y="179" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">⚠️ jediná třída, která prokazatelně snižuje úmrtnost</text><rect x="96" y="212" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="236" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">III. — blokátory K⁺ kanálů</text><text x="110" y="257" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">AMIODARON, sotalol · ⚠️ prodlužují QT → torsades</text><rect x="96" y="290" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="314" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">IV. — blokátory Ca²⁺ kanálů</text><text x="110" y="335" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">verapamil, diltiazem</text><rect x="96" y="368" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="392" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">MIMO KLASIFIKACI</text><text x="110" y="413" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">⚠️ ADENOSIN (poločas vteřiny) · digoxin · magnezium · ivabradin</text><line x1="58" y1="62" x2="58" y2="430" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="30" y="245" font-size="12" fill="#556964" transform="rotate(-90 30 245)" text-anchor="middle">I. až IV. třída</text><text x="450" y="456" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ KAŽDÉ ANTIARYTMIKUM MŮŽE ARYTMII TAKÉ VYVOLAT (studie CAST: třída IC po infarktu zvýšila úmrtnost). ⚠️ Amiodaron obsahuje jód</text><text x="450" y="471" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">→ tyreopatie, plicní fibróza, usazeniny v rohovce, poločas v týdnech.</text></svg>

## 68 · ACE inhibitory a antagonisté angiotensinu

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 368" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="368" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Proč po ACE inhibitoru kašel je a po sartanu ne</text><rect x="30" y="137" width="250" height="92" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="155" y="187" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">ACE = enzym se dvěma úkoly</text><line x1="286" y1="183" x2="462" y2="93" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="129" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">tvoří ANGIOTENSIN II</text><rect x="470" y="56" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="89" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">stah cév, aldosteron, remodelace → ⚠️ tohle chceme</text><text x="670" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">zastavit</text><line x1="286" y1="183" x2="462" y2="183" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="174" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">⚠️ rozkládá BRADYKININ</text><rect x="470" y="146" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="179" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">ACE inhibitor to zastaví taky → ⚠️ SUCHÝ KAŠEL,</text><text x="670" y="195" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">ANGIOEDÉM</text><line x1="286" y1="183" x2="462" y2="273" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="219" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">SARTAN blokuje až receptor AT1</text><rect x="470" y="236" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="277" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ bradykinin neovlivní → BEZ KAŠLE</text><text x="450" y="332" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Renoprotekce: uvolní se ODVODNÁ tepénka → klesne tlak v glomerulu → přestane se ničit. Mírný vzestup kreatininu na začátku je</text><text x="450" y="347" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">proto žádaný, ne selhání. ⚠️ KI: gravidita, oboustranná stenóza renálních tepen, hyperkalemie.</text></svg>

---

## 69 · Diuretika

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 399" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="399" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Nefron shora dolů — místo účinku určuje sílu i minerálovou poruchu</text><rect x="96" y="56" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="80" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#556964" text-anchor="start">PROXIMÁLNÍ TUBULUS</text><text x="110" y="101" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">acetazolamid (⚠️ acidóza, glaukom) · mannitol (mozkový edém)</text><rect x="96" y="134" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="158" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="start">HENLEOVA KLIČKA — vzestupné raménko</text><text x="110" y="179" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">⚠️ FUROSEMID — NEJSILNĚJŠÍ, ⚠️ ZTRÁCÍ VÁPNÍK, ototoxicita</text><rect x="96" y="212" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="236" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">DISTÁLNÍ TUBULUS</text><text x="110" y="257" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">THIAZIDY — ⚠️ ŠETŘÍ VÁPNÍK, ⚠️ hyperurikemie a hyperglykemie</text><rect x="96" y="290" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="314" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">SBĚRACÍ KANÁLEK</text><text x="110" y="335" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">⚠️ KALIUM ŠETŘÍCÍ: spironolakton, eplerenon, amilorid</text><line x1="58" y1="62" x2="58" y2="352" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="30" y="206" font-size="12" fill="#556964" transform="rotate(-90 30 206)" text-anchor="middle">průtok nefronem</text><text x="450" y="378" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">🔑 Furosemid vápník VYPLAVÍ, thiazid ho ZADRŽÍ. ⚠️ Všechna kromě posledního patra ztrácejí draslík — odtud konflikt s digoxinem.</text></svg>

---

## 70 · Blokátory kalciových kanálů

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 239" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="239" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Dvě skupiny, dva různé cíle — a z toho plyne, co se nesmí kombinovat</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#F4F7F5" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#C6D5D0" text-anchor="middle">DIHYDROPYRIDINY (-dipin)</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Amlodipin, nifedipin, lerkanidipin</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Míří hlavně na CÉVY → snižují tlak</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• NÚ: otoky kotníků, návaly, reflexní tep</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ HYPERPLAZIE GINGIVY</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">VERAPAMIL a DILTIAZEM</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Míří hlavně na SRDCE</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Bradykardie, slabší stah, zácpa</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ NEKOMBINOVAT s betablokátorem → AV blok</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ KI u srdečního selhání</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ VE ZDROJI JSOU DVĚ CHYBY: diltiazem je BENZOTHIAZEPIN (ne benzodiazepin) a verapamil se použije při kontraindikaci</text><text x="450" y="218" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">β-ANTAGONISTŮ (ne β-agonistů). ⚠️ Otoky kotníků nejsou z retence — diuretikum na ně nepomůže.</text></svg>

## 71 · Nitrity a nitráty

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 202" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="202" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Nerozšiřují hlavně věnčité tepny — rozšiřují ŽÍLY</text><rect x="30" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="107" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">NITRÁT uvolní NO</text><line x1="192" y1="101" x2="250" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="258" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="336" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">↑ cGMP → uvolnění</text><text x="336" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">hladkého svalu</text><line x1="421" y1="101" x2="479" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="487" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="564" y="88" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ hlavně ŽILY —</text><text x="564" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">krev se</text><text x="564" y="122" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">„zaparkuje"</text><line x1="650" y1="101" x2="708" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="716" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="793" y="88" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">↓ předtížení →</text><text x="793" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">srdce potřebuje</text><text x="793" y="122" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">MÍŇ KYSLÍKU</text><text x="450" y="166" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ TOLERANCE: nutný denní nitrátový interval 8–12 hodin. ⚠️ ABSOLUTNÍ KI: sildenafil a spol. — obojí zvyšuje cGMP, sečte se to a</text><text x="450" y="181" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">tlak spadne nezvratně. Nitroglycerin se ⚠️ nepolyká (first-pass), dává se pod jazyk vsedě.</text></svg>

---

## 72 · Farmakoterapie srdečního selhání

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 239" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="239" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Nejdůležitější rozlišení celé kardiologie</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">⭐ PRODLUŽUJÍ ŽIVOT — čtyři pilíře</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ACE inhibitor / sartan → nejlépe ARNI</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• BETABLOKÁTOR (jen 4 z nich, pomalu titrovat)</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Antagonista aldosteronu (spironolakton)</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• GLIFLOZIN — ⚠️ i u nediabetika</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#F4F7F5" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#C6D5D0" text-anchor="middle">JEN ULEVÍ — prognózu nemění</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Diuretika (furosemid)</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Digoxin</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Ivabradin (snižuje hospitalizace)</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ NEDÁVAT: NSA, verapamil, glitazony</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Proč tlumíme srdce, které nestačí? Protože chronická aktivace sympatiku a RAAS myokard přestavuje a ničí. Proto se</text><text x="450" y="218" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">betablokátor nasazuje pomalu a stav se může na začátku přechodně zhoršit.</text></svg>

---

## 73 · Farmakoterapie ischemické choroby srdeční

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 239" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="239" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">U chronické ICHS rozlišuj prognostickou a úlevovou léčbu</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">⭐ ZLEPŠUJÍ PROGNÓZU</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Antiagregancia</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Statin</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ACE inhibitor</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Betablokátor</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#F4F7F5" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#C6D5D0" text-anchor="middle">JEN ULEVÍ OD ANGÍNY</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Nitráty</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Ivabradin</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Trimetazidin, ranolazin</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Blokátory kalciových kanálů</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Betablokátor uleví tím, že zpomalí tep → prodlouží diastolu → věnčité tepny se plní právě v diastole. ⚠️ Duální antiagregace</text><text x="450" y="218" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">po stentu obvykle 12 měsíců — plánovanou extrakci v té době raději odložit, léky nevysazovat.</text></svg>

## 74 · Antihypertenziva

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 328" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="328" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Hypertenze se neléčí podle čísla, ale podle toho, kdo ji má</text><rect x="328" y="101" width="244" height="124" rx="14" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2"/><text x="450" y="167" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">JAKÝ LÉK PODLE PACIENTA?</text><rect x="26" y="56" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="83" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">diabetik / proteinurie → ACE</text><text x="139" y="99" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">inhibitor, sartan</text><line x1="258" y1="87" x2="322" y2="136" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="26" y="132" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="159" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">po infarktu → betablokátor +</text><text x="139" y="175" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">ACE inhibitor</text><line x1="258" y1="163" x2="322" y2="163" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="26" y="208" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="235" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">senior, izolovaná systolická</text><text x="139" y="251" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">→ blokátor Ca, thiazid</text><line x1="258" y1="239" x2="322" y2="190" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="56" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="91" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ dna → NE thiazid</text><line x1="642" y1="87" x2="578" y2="136" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="132" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="167" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ astma → NE betablokátor</text><line x1="642" y1="163" x2="578" y2="163" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="208" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="226" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ GRAVIDITA → methyldopa,</text><text x="761" y="243" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">labetalol, nifedipin — NE</text><text x="761" y="260" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">ACEI a sartany</text><line x1="642" y1="239" x2="578" y2="190" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="450" y="292" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Raději kombinovat nízké dávky několika léků v jedné tabletě než jeden vyhnat do maxima — účinek se sečte a nežádoucí účinky</text><text x="450" y="307" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">ne.</text></svg>

## 75 · Farmakoterapie aterosklerózy, hyperlipidemie

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 217" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="217" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Statin nesnižuje jen číslo — stabilizuje plát, který by praskl</text><rect x="30" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="107" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">STATIN blokuje</text><text x="107" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">HMG-CoA reduktázu</text><line x1="192" y1="101" x2="250" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="258" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="336" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">játra vyrobí míň</text><text x="336" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">cholesterolu</text><line x1="421" y1="101" x2="479" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="487" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="564" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">nahustí si LDL</text><text x="564" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">RECEPTORY</text><line x1="650" y1="101" x2="708" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="716" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="793" y="88" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">vytáhnou LDL z</text><text x="793" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">krve + ⚠️</text><text x="793" y="122" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">STABILIZUJE PLÁT</text><text x="450" y="166" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Myopatie až rabdomyolýza: bolest svalů + tmavá moč + vysoká kreatinkináza. ⚠️ Riziko roste s makrolidy, azoly, verapamilem,</text><text x="450" y="181" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">fibráty a grapefruitem (inhibitory CYP3A4). Ezetimib blokuje vstřebávání, inhibitory PCSK9 jsou injekční protilátky, fibráty</text><text x="450" y="196" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">míří na triacylglyceroly.</text></svg>

---

## 76 · Parenterální antikoagulancia

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 368" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="368" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Čím kratší řetězec, tím výhradněji míří na faktor Xa</text><rect x="30" y="137" width="250" height="92" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="155" y="179" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">HEPARIN sám neúčinkuje — ⚠️</text><text x="155" y="195" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">1000× zesílí ANTITROMBIN III</text><line x1="286" y1="183" x2="462" y2="93" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="129" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">NEFRAKCIONOVANÝ dlouhý řetězec</text><rect x="470" y="56" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ IIa i Xa · aPTT · ⚠️ antidotum PROTAMIN</text><line x1="286" y1="183" x2="462" y2="183" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="174" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">NÍZKOMOLEKULÁRNÍ enoxaparin</text><rect x="470" y="146" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="187" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ hlavně Xa · s.c. · bez rutinní monitorace</text><line x1="286" y1="183" x2="462" y2="273" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="219" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">FONDAPARINUX pentasacharid</text><rect x="470" y="236" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="277" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ výhradně Xa · ⚠️ NEDĚLÁ HIT</text><text x="450" y="332" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ HIT: protilátky proti komplexu heparinu a PF4 destičky ubírají A ZÁROVEŇ AKTIVUJÍ → málo destiček a přitom TROMBÓZY. ⚠️ Nikdy</text><text x="450" y="347" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">nepodávat destičky — vysadit heparin a nasadit jiné antikoagulans. ⚠️ Hepariny NEPROCHÁZEJÍ PLACENTOU → volba v graviditě.</text></svg>

## 77 · Perorální antikoagulancia

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 202" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="202" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Proč warfarin první dny paradoxně zvyšuje srážlivost</text><rect x="30" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="107" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">WARFARIN nasazen</text><line x1="192" y1="101" x2="250" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="258" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="336" y="88" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ nejdřív klesne</text><text x="336" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">PROTEIN C — má</text><text x="336" y="122" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">nejkratší poločas</text><line x1="421" y1="101" x2="479" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="487" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="564" y="88" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ pacient je</text><text x="564" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">přechodně</text><text x="564" y="122" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">PROTROMBOGENNÍ</text><line x1="650" y1="101" x2="708" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="716" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="793" y="80" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ proto se</text><text x="793" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">PŘEKRÝVÁ</text><text x="793" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">HEPARINEM, dokud</text><text x="793" y="130" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">INR nedosáhne cíle</text><text x="450" y="166" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">Blokuje γ-karboxylaci faktorů 🔑 II, VII, IX, X („1972") — ale i proteinů C a S. ⚠️ Zubařsky: PŘED BĚŽNOU EXTRAKCÍ SE NEVYSAZUJE,</text><text x="450" y="181" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">pokud je INR v rozmezí — řeší se místní hemostázou. DOAC: -xaban blokuje Xa, dabigatran trombin; ⚠️ nesmí u mechanické chlopně.</text></svg>

---

## 78 · Fibrinolytika, trombolytika, hemostatika

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 239" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="239" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Dvě protilehlé poloviny jedné otázky</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">TROMBOLÝZA — rozpustit sraženinu</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Alteplasa, tenektaplasa, streptokináza</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Aktivují plazminogen → plazmin → fibrin</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• STEMI bez katetrizace, plicní embolie</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ CMP jen do 4,5 hodiny</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">HEMOSTÁZA — zastavit krvácení</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Vitamin K · ⚠️ kyselina TRANEXAMOVÁ</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ DESMOPRESIN (vyplaví vWF a f. VIII)</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Místně: oxidovaná celulóza,</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• kolagenová houbička, fibrinové lepidlo, šití</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Ve zdroji je chyba: hemofilie A je VROZENÝ dědičný defekt faktoru VIII vázaný na X, ne „získaná geneticky". Extrakce u</text><text x="450" y="218" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">hemofilika se domlouvá předem s hematologem a ⚠️ nikdy NSA na bolest.</text></svg>

---

## 79 · Antiagregancia

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 239" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="239" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Proč se na tepnu a na žílu dávají úplně jiné léky</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">TEPNA — rychlý proud</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Sraženina hlavně z DESTIČEK</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• → ⚠️ ANTIAGREGANCIA</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Aspirin · klopidogrel, tikagrelor</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Inhibitory GPIIb/IIIa v katetrizaci</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#F4F7F5" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#C6D5D0" text-anchor="middle">ŽÍLA — pomalý proud</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Sraženina hlavně z FIBRINU</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• → ⚠️ ANTIKOAGULANCIA</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Warfarin, DOAC, hepariny</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Jiná indikace, jiné léky</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Aspirin acetyluje COX-1 IREVERZIBILNĚ; destička nemá jádro → účinek trvá 7–10 dní. ⚠️ Klopidogrel je proléčivo (CYP2C19 —</text><text x="450" y="218" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">omeprazol ho oslabí). ⚠️ Zubařsky: aspirin se před extrakcí NEVYSAZUJE.</text></svg>

---

## 80 · Inzulin, jeho analoga a glukagon

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 414" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="414" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Režim bazál-bolus napodobuje fyziologii</text><rect x="96" y="56" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="80" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="start">RYCHLÁ ANALOGA lispro, aspart</text><text x="110" y="101" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">nástup ~15 min → k jídlu (bolus)</text><rect x="96" y="134" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="158" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">KRÁTKÝ HUMÁNNÍ regular</text><text x="110" y="179" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">⚠️ nástup ~30 min → píchat s předstihem</text><rect x="96" y="212" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="236" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">STŘEDNÍ NPH</text><text x="110" y="257" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">překlenovací</text><rect x="96" y="290" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="314" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="start">DLOUHÁ ANALOGA glargin, degludek</text><text x="110" y="335" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">⚠️ bez vrcholu → bazál</text><line x1="58" y1="62" x2="58" y2="352" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="30" y="206" font-size="12" fill="#556964" transform="rotate(-90 30 206)" text-anchor="middle">delší účinek</text><text x="450" y="378" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Hlavní nebezpečí = HYPOGLYKEMIE. ⚠️ Betablokátor maskuje třes a bušení srdce, POCENÍ ZŮSTANE (cholinergní) a bývá jediným</text><text x="450" y="393" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">varováním. Glukagon i.m. u bezvědomí — ⚠️ a zároveň antidotum předávkování betablokátory.</text></svg>
