## 81 · Perorální antidiabetika

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 239" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="239" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Nejpraktičtější rozdělení antidiabetik</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ DĚLAJÍ HYPOGLYKEMII</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Sulfonylurea (glimepirid, gliklazid)</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• — uzavře KATP kanál a vyplaví inzulin</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• bez ohledu na glykemii</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Glinidy · (a samozřejmě inzulin)</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">NEDĚLAJÍ HYPOGLYKEMII</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⭐ METFORMIN — lék první volby</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Gliptiny · GLP-1 agonisté (hubnutí)</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ GLIFLOZINY — ochrana srdce a ledvin</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• Akarbóza, pioglitazon</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Metformin: vysadit před jodovou kontrastní látkou a při akutním stavu — laktátová acidóza. ⚠️ Glifloziny: glykosurie →</text><text x="450" y="218" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">mykotické infekce a euglykemická ketoacidóza.</text></svg>

## 82 · Principy antibiotické terapie

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 328" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="328" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Selektivní toxicita: zasáhnout to, co bakterie má a člověk ne</text><rect x="328" y="101" width="244" height="124" rx="14" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2"/><text x="450" y="158" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">BAKTERIÁLNÍ BUŇKA jeden</text><text x="450" y="175" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">obrázek na otázky 82–88</text><rect x="26" y="56" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="74" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">BUNĚČNÁ STĚNA ⚠️ člověk ji</text><text x="139" y="91" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">NEMÁ betalaktamy,</text><text x="139" y="108" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">glykopeptidy</text><line x1="258" y1="87" x2="322" y2="136" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="26" y="132" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="159" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">MEMBRÁNA polymyxiny,</text><text x="139" y="175" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">daptomycin</text><line x1="258" y1="163" x2="322" y2="163" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="26" y="208" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="235" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">RIBOZOM 30S aminoglykosidy,</text><text x="139" y="251" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">TETRACYKLINY</text><line x1="258" y1="239" x2="322" y2="190" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="56" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="83" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">RIBOZOM 50S makrolidy,</text><text x="761" y="99" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">KLINDAMYCIN, amfenikoly</text><line x1="642" y1="87" x2="578" y2="136" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="132" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="150" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">DNA chinolony → ⚠️</text><text x="761" y="167" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">TOPOIZOMERÁZA · rifampicin ·</text><text x="761" y="184" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">metronidazol</text><line x1="642" y1="163" x2="578" y2="163" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="208" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="235" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">KYSELINA LISTOVÁ sulfonamid</text><text x="761" y="251" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">+ trimethoprim</text><line x1="642" y1="239" x2="578" y2="190" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="450" y="292" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Koncentračně závislé (aminoglykosidy, chinolony) → velká dávka 1× denně. Časově závislé (betalaktamy) → rozdělit do více</text><text x="450" y="307" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">dávek. Rezistence: betalaktamázy, změna cíle, efluxní pumpy, nižší propustnost.</text></svg>

## 83 · Peniciliny, inhibitory betalaktamáz

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 458" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="458" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Řada od nejužšího po nejširší spektrum</text><rect x="30" y="182" width="250" height="92" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="155" y="215" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">PENICILINY blokáda stavby</text><text x="155" y="232" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">buněčné stěny ⚠️ jen na DĚLÍCÍ</text><text x="155" y="249" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">SE bakterie</text><line x1="286" y1="228" x2="462" y2="93" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="152" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">PŘIROZENÉ</text><rect x="470" y="56" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ PENICILIN V — lék volby u odontogenních infekcí</text><line x1="286" y1="228" x2="462" y2="183" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="196" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">PROTISTAFYLOKOKOVÉ</text><rect x="470" y="146" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="187" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">oxacilin — odolný vůči stafylokokové betalaktamáze</text><line x1="286" y1="228" x2="462" y2="273" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="242" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">AMINOPENICILINY</text><rect x="470" y="236" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="277" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">amoxicilin ⚠️ + kyselina klavulanová</text><line x1="286" y1="228" x2="462" y2="363" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="374" y="286" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle" stroke="#FFFFFF" stroke-width="3.4" paint-order="stroke">UREIDOPENICILINY</text><rect x="470" y="326" width="400" height="74" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="670" y="367" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">piperacilin + tazobaktam → Pseudomonas</text><text x="450" y="422" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Inhibitor betalaktamázy sám skoro neúčinkuje — obětuje se enzymu. ⚠️ Ampicilinový exantém u infekční mononukleózy NENÍ</text><text x="450" y="437" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">alergie. ⚠️ Člověk buněčnou stěnu nemá → hlavním rizikem je alergie, ne toxicita.</text></svg>

---

## 84 · Cefalosporiny, karbapenemy, monobaktamy

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 492" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="492" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Čím vyšší generace, tím míň G+ a víc G−</text><rect x="96" y="56" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="80" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">I. generace — cefazolin, cefalexin</text><text x="110" y="101" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">grampozitivní · chirurgická profylaxe</text><rect x="96" y="134" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="158" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">II. — cefuroxim</text><text x="110" y="179" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">+ Haemophilus, respirační infekce</text><rect x="96" y="212" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="236" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="start">III. — ceftriaxon, ceftazidim</text><text x="110" y="257" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">⚠️ gramnegativní · PROSTUP DO CNS → meningitidy</text><rect x="96" y="290" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="314" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">IV.–V. — cefepim, ceftarolin</text><text x="110" y="335" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">⚠️ ceftarolin jako jediný i na MRSA</text><rect x="96" y="368" width="748" height="66" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="110" y="392" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="start">KARBAPENEMY — meropenem</text><text x="110" y="413" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">⚠️ nejširší spektrum → REZERVA, dál už není kam ustoupit</text><line x1="58" y1="62" x2="58" y2="430" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="30" y="245" font-size="12" fill="#556964" transform="rotate(-90 30 245)" text-anchor="middle">generace</text><text x="450" y="456" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ ŽÁDNÝ cefalosporin nepůsobí na ENTEROKOKY ani na ATYPICKÉ patogeny. ⚠️ Aztreonam (monobaktam) je natolik odlišný, že ho lze</text><text x="450" y="471" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">podat i při alergii na penicilin.</text></svg>

---

## 85 · Aminoglykosidy, chinolony

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 239" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="239" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Dvě baktericidní, koncentračně závislé skupiny — každá s vlastní toxicitou</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">AMINOGLYKOSIDY</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ 30S ribozom, ale BAKTERICIDNÍ (výjimka)</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Nevstřebají se ústy → jen injekčně</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ NEFROTOXICITA (vratná)</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ OTOTOXICITA — často TRVALÁ</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">CHINOLONY</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ TOPOIZOMERÁZA II (gyráza) a IV</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Ruptura Achillovy šlachy</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ KI u dětí a v graviditě (chrupavky)</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Chelatace s Ca, Mg, Fe → ne s mlékem</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ VE ZDROJI JE CHYBA: chinolony neblokují DNA-polymerázu, ale TOPOIZOMERÁZU (DNA-gyrázu). ⚠️ Aminoglykosidy se dávkují 1× denně</text><text x="450" y="218" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">— vysoký vrchol zabíjí líp a dlouhá pauza chrání ledvinu.</text></svg>

## 86 · Linkosamidy, glykopeptidy, polymyxiny

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 328" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="328" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Proč právě klindamycin u infekcí čelisti</text><rect x="328" y="101" width="244" height="124" rx="14" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2"/><text x="450" y="150" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">KLINDAMYCIN zubařsky</text><text x="450" y="167" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">nejdůležitější z téhle</text><text x="450" y="184" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">trojice</text><rect x="26" y="56" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="91" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Výborný průnik do KOSTI</text><line x1="258" y1="87" x2="322" y2="136" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="26" y="132" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="167" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ Účinný na ANAEROBY</text><line x1="258" y1="163" x2="322" y2="163" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="26" y="208" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="139" y="235" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">→ odontogenní absces,</text><text x="139" y="251" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">osteomyelitida čelisti</text><line x1="258" y1="239" x2="322" y2="190" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="56" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="83" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">→ ⚠️ alternativa při alergii</text><text x="761" y="99" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">na penicilin</text><line x1="642" y1="87" x2="578" y2="136" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="132" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="159" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ RIZIKO: pseudomembranózní</text><text x="761" y="175" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">kolitida (C. difficile)</text><line x1="642" y1="163" x2="578" y2="163" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="648" y="208" width="226" height="62" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="761" y="235" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">léčí se ⚠️ vankomycinem</text><text x="761" y="251" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">PERORÁLNĚ nebo fidaxomicinem</text><line x1="642" y1="239" x2="578" y2="190" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><text x="450" y="292" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">Vankomycin: ⚠️ jen G+, lék volby u MRSA; ⚠️ perorálně se nevstřebá — proto se tak podává právě na klostridiovou kolitidu. ⚠️</text><text x="450" y="307" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">„Red man syndrome" při rychlé infuzi není alergie. Kolistin = rezerva na multirezistentní G−, nefrotoxický.</text></svg>

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 202" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, účinek v celé farmakologii</text><rect x="30" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="107" y="97" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">TETRACYKLIN se</text><text x="107" y="113" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">váže na VÁPNÍK</text><line x1="192" y1="101" x2="250" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="258" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="336" y="88" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">ukládá se do</text><text x="336" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">rostoucí kosti a</text><text x="336" y="122" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="middle">ZUBU</text><line x1="421" y1="101" x2="479" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="487" y="58" width="154" height="86" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="564" y="88" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ ŠEDOHNĚDÉ</text><text x="564" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">ZBARVENÍ UVNITŘ</text><text x="564" y="122" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">skloviny a dentinu</text><line x1="650" y1="101" x2="708" y2="101" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="2" marker-end="url(#hrot)"/><rect x="716" y="58" VYBĚLIT</text><text x="793" y="105" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">— barvivo není na</text><text x="793" y="122" font-size="13.5" font-weight="400" fill="#5B1E16" text-anchor="middle">povrchu</text><text x="450" y="166" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ VE ZDROJI JE CHYBA: tetracykliny NEBLOKUJÍ buněčnou stěnu, ale RIBOZOM (30S). ⚠️ KI do 8 let a v graviditě. ⚠️ Nezapíjet</text><text x="450" y="181" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">mlékem (chelatace). Amfenikoly: ⚠️ aplastická anemie nezávislá na dávce a gray baby syndrom.</text></svg>

## 88 · Makrolidy

<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" viewBox="0 0 900 224" width="100%" style="max-width:100%;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px 0" font-family="Calibri, Carlito, Arial, sans-serif"><defs><marker id="hrot" markerWidth="9" markerHeight="9" refX="8" refY="3" orient="auto"><path d="M0,0 L0,6 L8,3 z" fill="#1B6B5F"/></marker></defs><rect x="0" y="0" width="900" height="224" rx="8" fill="#FFFFFF" stroke="#C6D5D0" stroke-width="1.4"/><text x="450" y="22" font-size="14.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">Největší úskalí makrolidů nejsou nežádoucí účinky, ale interakce</text><rect x="30" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#FBEEE9" stroke="#9C3628" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="234" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">ERYTHROMYCIN a KLARITHROMYCIN</text><text x="46" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ SILNÉ INHIBITORY CYP3A4</text><text x="46" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• → ⚠️ statin (rabdomyolýza), warfarin,</text><text x="46" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• cyklosporin, karbamazepin</text><text x="46" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Erythromycin dráždí žaludek (motilin)</text><rect x="463" y="56" width="407" height="125" rx="7" fill="#E7F0ED" stroke="#1B6B5F" stroke-width="1.6"/><text x="666" y="82" font-size="13.5" font-weight="600" fill="#1B6B5F" text-anchor="middle">AZITHROMYCIN</text><text x="479" y="108" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• 🔑 CYP3A4 prakticky neinhibuje</text><text x="479" y="123" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• → volba u polymorbidního pacienta</text><text x="479" y="138" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• ⚠️ Velmi dlouhý tkáňový poločas</text><text x="479" y="153" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#1A2421" text-anchor="start">• → třídenní kúra působí ještě dny</text><text x="450" y="203" font-size="12" font-weight="400" fill="#9C3628" text-anchor="middle">⚠️ 50S ribozom, bakteriostatické, pokrývají atypické patogeny a jsou alternativou při alergii na penicilin. ⚠️ Prodlužují QT.</text></svg>
